Every PASS line of `checklist.txt` (4105) corresponds to one of the C.6 checks; no address, offset, operand, coordinate, slot, parity or coverage check failed.

## D.2 SASS lowering of the kernel (D11; `resources.txt`, `sass_gemm.txt`, `ptx.txt`)

Static shape: the GEMM kernel (`device_kernel<GemmUniversal<...>>`) is 3224 SASS instructions; the embedded PTX for it is 2800 instructions (`ptx.txt:45-4343`, 99 distinct mnemonic strings, 214 predicated) with `.maxntid 256`, `.minnctapersm 1`, one `.param .align 128 .b8 [2048]`, `.reg .pred %p<248>; .b16 %rs<16>; .b32 %r<1635>; .b64 %rd<55>` plus 147 registers declared inside inline-asm scopes. Resource usage of the pristine binary: `REG:68 STACK:0 SHARED:1024 LOCAL:0 CONSTANT[0]:2944`. Addresses below are the byte offsets printed by `cuobjdump` (`/*3a80*/`).

PTX-to-SASS dictionary for every primitive of Section 7.2 and B.5 (counts are for the whole kernel; "uniform" means a uniform-datapath instruction with `UR` operands that issues once per warp regardless of how many lanes reach it):

| PTX (Section 7 / B.5) | SASS | Sites | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tcgen05.mma.cta_group::2.kind::f16` | `UTCHMMA.2CTA gdesc[URa], gdesc[URb], tmem[UR11], tmem[UR32], idesc[UR33], UPx` | 4: `0x3a80`, `0x3ac0`, `0x3b00`, `0x3b10` | uniform, unpredicated; the surrounding `elect.sync` is folded away. `UR11 = T + 128*acc_stage`, `UR32 = 0`, `UR33 = 0x10200010`. The scale-c operand is the uniform predicate `UP2` on the first MMA (cleared per tile at `0x3860`, set to true at `0x3a90`, right after that MMA) and the constant-true `UPT` on the other three; the eight zero mask registers of the PTX do not appear as operands |
| `tcgen05.commit ... multicast::cluster` | `UTCBAR.2CTA.MULTICAST [URaddr], URZ, URmask` | 2: `0x3b20` (mainloop `empty[s]`, `UR27 = 0xF`), `0x3b70` (accumulator `full[acc]`, `UR36 = 0x3` or `0xC`) | uniform; the masks are run-time registers computed from `%cluster_ctaid` (`0x1050-0x10f0`, `0x3530-0x3620`); the mainloop commit is the instruction after the fourth `UTCHMMA` |
| `tcgen05.alloc.cta_group::2 ... 512` | `UTCATOMSWS.2CTA.FIND_AND_SET.ALIGN UP0, UR4, UR4` (`UR4 = 0x10`) inside a 69-instruction sequence `0x3020-0x3460` | 2 sites (`0x30c0`, `0x3120`; retry with `NANOSLEEP 0x64`) | one allocation unit = 32 TMEM columns (`0x10` units = 512 columns). The atomic is executed by the elected lane of the **even** CTA only; the result is stored locally (`STS [UR29+0x180], R9<<5`) and forwarded to the peer's `smem+384` with `STAS` (st.async), which completes a barrier in the peer's system-reserved shared memory at `(rank<<24) + 0x48`; bookkeeping words at `+0x50/+0x54/+0x58` (`ATOMS.OR/XOR`); the peer's elected lane waits, rescales and acknowledges; `WARPSYNC.ALL` at `0x32d0` (alloc-internal reconvergence point, target of the `0x3070` and `0x3090` branches) and at `0x3440` (the position of the following `bar.warp.sync -1`, also the target of `@P0 BRA 0x3440` at `0x3310`); then `BAR.ARV 0x6, 0xa0` (`0x3460`) |
| `tcgen05.dealloc.cta_group::2 ... 512` | `UTCATOMSWS.AND URZ, UR5`, `UR5 = ~(0xffff << (T >> 5))` | 1: `0x4140` | in both CTAs; preceded by two ptxas-inserted consistency checks on the reserved words (`0x4010-0x40c0`) whose failure paths `CALL` stubs containing `BPT.TRAP 0x1` (`0xc860`, `0xc890`) although the PTX has no `trap`; followed by `ATOMS.AND` clears and `EXIT` (`0x4220`) |
| `tcgen05.relinquish_alloc_permit` | no `UTC*` mnemonic; `UVIRTCOUNT.DEALLOC.SMPOOL 0x80` (`0x3c70`) and an `ELECT`-guarded `STS.U8 [(rank<<24)+0x5c], 1` (`0x3cb0`) bracket the `PREEXIT` | 1 | attribution **inferred** (the only otherwise unexplained instructions at the PTX position); ptxas emitted `UVIRTCOUNT` *before* `PREEXIT`, i.e. swapped relative to the PTX order (`launch_dependents` at `ptx.txt:1873`, `relinquish` at `:1876`) |
| `griddepcontrol.launch_dependents` | `PREEXIT` | 1: `0x3c80` (warp 0, unpredicated) | |
| `griddepcontrol.wait` | `ACQBULK` | 3: `0x1750` (warp 2), `0x2b30` (rank-0 warp 1), `0x4280` (warp 3, not executed for beta = 0) | |
| `tcgen05.ld.sync.aligned.32x32b.x16.b32` | `LDTM.x16 R4, tmem[UR4 + 0x10*e]` | 8: `0x7bf0`, `0x8370`, `0x8ab0`, `0x9230`, `0x99f0`, `0xa170`, `0xa930`, `0xb070` | `UR4 = T + ((32w) << 16) + 128*acc_stage` (`R2UR` at `0x7bb0`), the `16e` term is the immediate; destination registers `R4..R19` |
| `tcgen05.wait::ld.sync.aligned` | **no instruction**: the dependency is carried by the control-word scoreboards | (`LDTM` at `0xb070` sets SB1; the `SYNCS.ARRIVE` at `0xb090` waits on SB1) | for subtiles 0..6 the first `FMUL R4` waits on the scoreboard set by its `LDTM` (`0x7c10` waits on SB4, `0x8380` and later on SB1), and the `STS` of a subtile carry a read-scoreboard so that the next `LDTM` waits for them (WAR). The absence of any wait opcode between `LDTM` and its consumers is measured; the scoreboard decode of the control words is inferred from the encoding |
| A load `cp.async.bulk.tensor.3d.cta_group::2 ... multicast::cluster` | `UTMALDG.3D.MULTICAST.2CTA [UR16], [URtmap], URmask, desc[UR4]` | 2: `0x1c30` (8-k-tile loop: tensormap `UR24`, mask `UR15`), `0x1f10` (120-k-tile loop: tensormap `UR22`, mask `UR21`) | uniform, unpredicated (elect folded); the multicast mask `0x5 << x` is computed once into `UR33` (`UMOV UR33, 0x5` at `0x0f20`, `USHF.L.U32 UR33, UR33, UR5, URZ` at `0x0f80`) and placed in bits [16,32) of the mask register by `UPRMT URmask, URZ, 0x5410, UR33`: `UR15` at `0x1a80` for the 8-k-tile loop, `UR21` at `0x1d50` for the 120-k-tile loop (the only two such `UPRMT` in the kernel; measured, `sass_gemm.txt:851/905/941/997`); `desc[UR4] = {0x0, 0x10000000}` is the cache hint `0x1000000000000000`; `UR16 = base + 0x8400 + (s << 14) + 8192y`, coordinates `{64q, 128tm + 64y, L_idx}`; the z operand is `UR20` (A; copied to `UR12` for the B load by `UMOV UR12, UR20` at `0x1c10`/`0x1ee0`, slot reading inferred from the `UR18 = 64q` / `UMOV UR10, UR18` companions): `S2UR UR20, SR_CTAID.Z` (`0x0f10`) for the launch tile, overwritten by `@UP0 UMOV UR20, UR4` (`0x20e0`, `UR4` = response `word1 >> 16` = the `get_first_ctaid` z bound to `work_tile_info.L_idx`, `sm100_tile_scheduler.hpp:409-436`) for every CLC tile; 0 for every tile of this run (measured: `c2 = 0` in all 128 K4a and 256 K4b records, raw `L_idx = 0` in all 1024 K5b and 4096 K5c records) |
| B load `cp.async.bulk.tensor.3d.cta_group::2 ...` | `UTMALDG.3D.2CTA [UR8], [URtmap], desc[UR4]` | 2: `0x1c50` (tensormap `UR22`), `0x1f20` (tensormap `UR14`) | both producer loops are rolled, one copy each: loop 1 counter runs `-7..1` (8 iterations), loop 2 `8..k_tiles` (120); loop 2 body = 31 instructions (`0x1d60-0x1f40`) |
| C load `cp.async.bulk.tensor.3d.shared::cluster.global ... L2::cache_hint` (beta != 0) | `@!UP0 UTMALDG.3D` | 32: `0x50c0-0x6450` | compiled, never executed here; the only TMA instructions that keep an election predicate (`ELECT` at `0x4e50` -> `VOTEU.ALL`) |
| D store `cp.async.bulk.tensor.3d.global.shared::cta.bulk_group` | `UTMASTG.3D [URtuple], [UR72]` | 32: 4 per subtile (`0x7f70`, `0x7fe0`, `0x8000`, `0x8030`, ..., `0xb4e0`) | one uniform, unpredicated instruction per PTX store, issued once per warp whatever the lane count (K4b measured 32 active lanes at every issue); reached by warp 4 only through `@P1 BRA` (`P1 = (tid & 0x60) != 0`, `0x6640`/`0x6720`); `UR72` (subtile 0; `UR70/UR68/UR66/UR64/UR62/UR60/UR30` for subtiles 1-7, all eight 64-bit pairs computed once from `LDCU.64 UR6, c[0x0][0x348]` at `0x6630` by `UIADD3 URn, ..., UR6, 0x680` + `UIADD3.X` at `0x67f0-0x6be0`, never rewritten before the stores) `= c[0x0][0x348] + 0x680`, the D descriptor's generic address (measured, `sass_gemm.txt`) |
| `cp.async.bulk.commit_group` | `UTMACMDFLUSH` | 8 (`0x8040` ... `0xb4f0`) | |
| `cp.async.bulk.wait_group.read 1` / `.read 0` | `DEPBAR.LE SB0, 0x1` / `DEPBAR.LE SB0, 0x0` | 8 (`0x8050` ...) / 1 (`0xb760`) | the `.read 0` of `store_tail` is present but not reached for beta = 0 (D15); the other two `DEPBAR.LE SB1, 0x36` sit in the alloc sequence |
| `prefetch.tensormap` | `UTMACCTL.PF` | 4: `0x0310`/`0x0320` (A, B by warp 1), `0x03d0`/`0x03e0` (C, D by warp 3) | descriptors stay in parameter space (`c[0x0][0x348] + 0x80/0x180/0x580/0x680`) |
| `mbarrier.init` | `SYNCS.EXCH.64 [UR29 + off], R` with init word `{(0x100000 - count) << 1, (0x100000 - count) << 11}` | 43 (`0x0630-0x0d80`) | offsets exactly as Section 6.2 and counts exactly as the Section 7.2 table (the CLC empty count is a run-time `UMOV UR4, 0x3a0; USEL UR4, UR4, 0x320, UP3` at `0x09a0`/`0x09c0` on `is_producer_load_needed`); 42 of 43 are unpredicated uniform instructions behind the warp-index branch (the `elect.sync` guard is folded); only the `tmem_dealloc` init keeps `VOTEU.ALL UP0; @!UP0 SYNCS.EXCH.64` |
| `fence.mbarrier_init.release.cluster` | **no instruction** | 0 | seven bare `FENCE.VIEW.ASYNC.S` precede the init groups (`0x0620`, ..., `0x0d70`); attributing them to the init lowering is inferred |

| `mbarrier.test_wait.parity` | `SYNCS.PHASECHK.TRANS64 P, [addr], R` (no `.TRYWAIT`) | 2: `0x2eb0`, `0x2f70` (CLC `producer_tail`) | the two PTX wait forms stay distinguishable in SASS |
| `mapa.shared::cluster` | **no instruction**: byte 3 of the address is replaced by the rank, `PRMT`/`UPRMT dst, rank, 0x654, addr` | 25 | compiler-side confirmation that the CTA rank lives in address bits [24,32) |
| `mbarrier.arrive.shared::cluster` (remote) | `SYNCS.ARRIVE.TRANS64.RED.A1T0 RZ, [addr], RZ` | 27 | |
| `mbarrier.arrive.shared::cta` (local) | `SYNCS.ARRIVE.TRANS64.A1T0 RZ, [addr], RZ` | 2: `0x1910` (throttle), `0x1cb0` (load-order, predicated off) | |
| `mbarrier.arrive.expect_tx.shared::cta ... 49152` | `@P0 SYNCS.ARRIVE.TRANS64 RZ, [UR17], R2` (`R2 = 0xc000`) | 2: `0x1af0`, `0x1dc0` | per-lane predicate `P0` = elected lane and leader and warp 2; the address is the unmasked local `full[s]` |
| `mbarrier.arrive.expect_tx.shared::cluster ... 16` (CLC arming) | `@!P2 SYNCS.ARRIVE.TRANS64.RED RZ, [PRMT(lane)], R9` (`R9 = 0x10`) | 1: `0x2cd0` (plus the same form with 4 bytes in the alloc handshake, `0x3260`) | lanes 0..3 |
| `mbarrier.expect_tx.shared::cta ... 8192` (C load) | `SYNCS.ARRIVE.TRANS64.RED.A0TR` | 8 (not executed) | |

| `query_cancel.is_canceled` / `get_first_ctaid` | bit-field extraction of the `LDS.128` response: `is_canceled` = bit 0 of word 2 (`LOP3.LUT P, RZ, Rword2, 0x1`); `x0 = word0`, `y0 = word1 & 0xffff`, `z0 = word1 >> 16` | 5 `LDS.128`: `0x1fb0`, `0x2db0`, `0x36e0`, `0x4660`, `0x6f70` | the response layout is inferred from the extraction code (x/y/z assignment follows the PTX operand order); in the MMA warp only the valid bit survives (`get_first_ctaid` eliminated as dead) |
