Write the peer mask as `& 0xFEFFFFFF` in C++ (nvcc folds it to `0xFEFFFFF8`, D.6 item 2); write the blocking wait and the election with PTX labels/registers inside `{ }` so they can be inlined many times.

### E.6.3 Prologue (all 256 threads of every CTA; 7.3, B.2)

1. `warp_idx = warp_idx_sync()`, `lane = threadIdx.x & 31`, `lane_pred = elect_one_sync()` (the prologue election, reused only where the baseline reuses it: the prefetches (kernel `:440, :443`), the producer's `is_leader_lane` (`:465`) and the `tmem_dealloc` init (`:564-565`); the pipeline `mbarrier.init` groups of step 3 are NOT gated by it in the baseline: each `initialize_barrier_array*_aligned` helper performs its own `elect_one_sync()` (`barrier.h:123, 134, 146`; B.2 step 12), so the explicit kernel issues one fresh W2 per init group (4 in the 33-init variant, 6 in the 43-init variant); reusing `lane_pred` for the inits would be functionally equivalent and still meet the soft `elect.sync >= 9` of E.7.4, but is a PTX-level deviation to disclose), `rank`, `V = rank & 1`, `is_leader`, `peer`, `is_cta0` (kernel `:422-430`).
2. `alpha = params.alpha; beta = params.beta;` (every thread; no stride/pointer tests: the explicit struct has no pointer path), `k_tiles = (params.k + 63) >> 6`. Prefetch: `if (warp_idx == 1 && lane_pred) { prefetch(&params.tma_a); prefetch(&params.tma_b); }`, `if (warp_idx == 3 && lane_pred) { prefetch(&params.tma_c); prefetch(&params.tma_d); }` in every CTA (kernel `:439-445`; C kept for the 4-`UTMACCTL.PF` parity, D.7).
3. Barrier initialisation in the baseline's constructor order, each group by the elected lane of its initialising warp, each followed by W14 from **all** threads: warp 0 mainloop `full[0..7]` (1) / `empty[0..7]` (2) (`:457-472`; `sm100_pipeline.hpp:555-573`); [warp 1 `epi_load` 4+4 (32/128) and warp 3 `load_order` 2 (32): **omitted** in the recommended 33-init variant, kept only for 43-init parity]; warp 4 CLC `full[0..1]` (1) / `empty[0..1]` (800) (`:501-518`); warp 5 accumulator `full[0..3]` (1) / `empty[0..3]` (256) (`:520-536`); warp 3 throttle `full[0..1]`/`empty[0..1]` (32/32) (`:538-550`, constructed *after* the accumulator pipeline); warp 0 `lane_pred` `tmem_dealloc` (32) (`:562-565`, no fence of its own). Fence count: 4 in the 33-init variant, 6 in the 43-init variant (`trace_out/ptx.txt` has 6). Recommended: 33 inits, the unused 6.2 slots left as reserved space (D.7 accepts either; E.10 question 6).
4. `cluster_arrive_relaxed()` (`:580`). 5. Warp-uniform setup: `(x, y)`, masks, initial tile `tm = 2*(blockIdx.y >> 1) + (blockIdx.x & 1)`, `tn = 2*(blockIdx.x >> 1) + (blockIdx.y & 1)` (`swizzle_and_rasterize` with divisor 0 + `possibly_transpose_work_tile(AlongN)`, `sm100_tile_scheduler.hpp:365-371, 666-677`), pipeline states (E.6.5); no smem touched. 6. `cluster_wait()` (`:615`); no remote barrier operation before this point. 7. Dispatch in the baseline's branch order (`:617, 681, 726, 807, 869`): `if (warp_idx == 2) producer_role(); else if (warp_idx == 1 && is_cta0) scheduler_role(); else if (warp_idx == 0) mma_role(); else if (warp_idx >= 4) epilogue_role();` warp 3 (all CTAs) and warp 1 of ranks 1-3 fall through and exit (7.3 step 7, 7.10).

Warp-convergence contract: `elect.sync`, every `tcgen05.*.sync.aligned`, `bar.warp.sync`, `barrier.cluster.*.aligned`, `bar.sync/arrive` need the whole warp (or the named-barrier population) converged; role functions contain no lane-divergent `return`, and `if (is_cta0)` / `if (is_leader)` are warp-uniform by construction. `tcgen05.alloc` preconditions (`tmem_allocator_sm100.hpp:124-133`): both CTAs of the pair issue it from a fully active warp 0 with the *same* `dst` offset (`OFF_TMEM_BASE_PTR` = 384); `dealloc` from the same warp with the same column count.

### E.6.4 Role functions (7.4-7.7, B.3; pseudo-code fixes the order, the KB fixes the values)

**`producer_role` (warp 2, every CTA; 7.5, B.3.1).** Invariants: `a_dst_base = smem_base + OFF_SMEM_A + 8192*y`, `b_dst_base = smem_base + OFF_SMEM_B`, `full_masked_base = smem_base & kPeerBitMask`, `empty_base = smem_base + OFF_MAIN_EMPTY`, `is_leader_lane = lane_pred && is_leader`.
```
griddep_wait();
do {
  a_row = 128*tm + 64*y;  b_col = 128*tn + 64*x;  q = 0;
  if (is_cta0) { mbar_wait(throttle_empty[thr_idx], thr_phase); mbar_arrive_local(throttle_full[thr_idx]); advance<2>(thr_idx, thr_phase); }   // :632-636
  load_ktiles(min(8, k_tiles));  load_ktiles(k_tiles - 8);          // two code copies -> two rolled loops (8 + 120), load_order arrive skipped (beta = 0)
  __syncwarp();                                                      // :664
  valid = clc_consume(tm_next, tn_next);  tm = tm_next; tn = tn_next; // consume AFTER the loads (:670-671)
} while (valid);
for (i = 0; i < 8; ++i) { mbar_wait(empty_base + 8*prod_idx, prod_phase); advance<8>(prod_idx, prod_phase); }   // load_tail, sm90_pipeline.hpp:445-454
```
`load_ktiles(n)` (`sm100_mma_warpspecialized.hpp:589-629`): `tok = mbar_try_wait(empty[prod_idx], prod_phase)` (:604); `#pragma unroll 1` (:607) `while (n > 0) { if (!tok) mbar_wait(...) (:610); if (is_leader_lane) mbar_arrive_expect_tx_local(smem_base + 8*prod_idx, 49152); s = prod_idx; advance<8>(prod_idx, prod_phase); tok = mbar_try_wait(empty[prod_idx], prod_phase) (unconditional, :617); if (elect_one_sync()) { tma_load_2sm_mcast_3d(&params.tma_a, full_masked_base + 8*s, tma_a_mask, hint, a_dst_base + 16384*s, 64*q, a_row, 0); tma_load_2sm_3d(&params.tma_b, full_masked_base + 8*s, hint, b_dst_base + 8192*s, 64*q, b_col, 0); } (:619-622, one election for the pair); --n; ++q; }`.

**`scheduler_role` (warp 1 of rank 0; 7.4, B.3.4).**
```
griddep_wait();
do {
  mbar_wait(throttle_full[thc_idx], thc_phase); mbar_arrive_remote(throttle_empty[thc_idx], 0); advance<2>(thc_idx, thc_phase);   // consumer_wait + consumer_release, all lanes
  mbar_wait(clc_empty[clp_idx], clp_phase);                                                           // producer_acquire, sm100_pipeline.hpp:1095-1104
  mbar_arrive_expect_tx_remote_pred(clc_full_addr(clp_idx), lane, lane < 4, 16);                     // lanes 0..3 arm CTAs 0..3
  if (elect_one_sync()) clc_try_cancel(smem_base + OFF_CLC_RESPONSE + 16*clp_idx, clc_full_addr(clp_idx));
  advance<2>(clp_idx, clp_phase);
  valid = clc_consume(tm_next, tn_next);  tm = tm_next; tn = tn_next;                                 // this warp consumes too (its 32 are in the 800)
} while (valid);
for (i = 0; i < 2; ++i) { done = mbar_test_wait(clc_empty[clp_idx], clp_phase, 1); if (!done) mbar_wait(clc_empty[clp_idx], clp_phase); advance<2>(clp_idx, clp_phase); }   // producer_tail :1041-1050
```

**`mma_role` (warp 0, every CTA; 7.6, B.3.2).** Setup: `tmem_alloc_2sm(smem_base + OFF_TMEM_BASE_PTR, 512)`; `__syncwarp()`; `named_bar_arrive(6, 160)`; `T = *reinterpret_cast<volatile uint32_t*>(smem + OFF_TMEM_BASE_PTR)` (ordering of this load after the clobber-less `tcgen05.alloc` asm (`tmem_allocator_sm100.hpp:138-141`) is guaranteed by the `"memory"` clobber of the intervening `bar.arrive 6,160` (W19, `barrier.h:308`), which W19 must keep per E.6.2; CUTLASS itself does a plain load here, `sm100_gemm_tma_warpspecialized.hpp:731`; the `volatile` is an added, harmless guard, not a copy of the source); `desc_lo_a = kSmemDescLo0 | (((smem_base + OFF_SMEM_A) >> 4) & 0x3FFF)`, `desc_lo_b = kSmemDescLo0 | (((smem_base + OFF_SMEM_B) >> 4) & 0x3FFF)` (expected `0x10880` / `0x12880`; the `& 0x3FFF` drops the rank bits so both CTAs hold identical descriptors, `mma_sm100_desc.hpp:754-755`).
```
do {
  valid = clc_consume(tm_next, tn_next);                        // BEFORE computing the current tile (:742-750)
  st = accp_idx;
  if (is_leader) {
    tok = (k_tiles <= 0) ? 1 : mbar_try_wait(full[cons_idx], cons_phase);   // consumer_try_wait :670-671 (skip logic: sm90_pipeline.hpp:590-597)
    scale_c = 0;  mbar_wait(acc_empty[st], accp_phase);  tmem_c = T + 128*st;  k = k_tiles;   // :676-678
    #pragma unroll 1                                                          // :680
    while (k > 0) {
      if (!tok) mbar_wait(full[cons_idx], cons_phase);                        // :684
      rs = cons_idx; advance<8>(cons_idx, cons_phase); --k;
      tok = (k <= 0) ? 1 : mbar_try_wait(full[cons_idx], cons_phase);         // :694-696, skipped on the last k-tile
      lo_a = desc_lo_a + 1024*rs;  lo_b = desc_lo_b + 512*rs;                 // 32-bit low-word arithmetic, mma_sm100_desc.hpp:861-874
      #pragma unroll                                                          // :699
      for (kb = 0; kb < 4; ++kb) { if (elect_one_sync()) umma_f16_2sm(tmem_c, desc64(kSmemDescHi, lo_a + 2*kb), desc64(kSmemDescHi, lo_b + 2*kb), kInstrDesc, scale_c); scale_c = 1; }   // :706
      if (elect_one_sync()) umma_commit_mcast_2sm(empty[rs], 0xF);            // consumer_release :708
    }
    if (elect_one_sync()) umma_commit_mcast_2sm(acc_full[st], acc_mask);      // producer_commit :771
  }
  advance<4>(accp_idx, accp_phase);  tm = tm_next; tn = tn_next;               // both CTAs :773-775
} while (valid);
griddep_launch_dependents();  tmem_relinquish_2sm();                           // :781, :784
if (is_leader) for (i = 0; i < 4; ++i) { mbar_wait(acc_empty[accp_idx], accp_phase); advance<4>(accp_idx, accp_phase); }   // producer_tail :789
if (!is_leader) mbar_arrive_remote(dealloc, peer);  mbar_wait(dealloc, 0);  if (is_leader) mbar_arrive_remote(dealloc, peer);   // handshake :794-796
tmem_dealloc_2sm(T, 512);                                                      // :804
```
`desc64(hi, lo) = (uint64_t(hi) << 32) | lo`, formed at the asm boundary so that ptxas can keep `lo` uniform and rematerialise `hi` (D.2); never a table of per-stage descriptors, never 64-bit stage arithmetic.

**`epilogue_role` (warps 4-7, every CTA; 7.7, B.3.3).** Setup: `named_bar_sync(6, 160)`; `T` read (ordered after `bar.sync 6,160` by its `"memory"` clobber, W18, `barrier.h:287`; the source does a plain load at `sm100_gemm_tma_warpspecialized.hpp:872`; `volatile` optional, harmless); `w = warp_idx - 4`, `l = lane`; `tmem_lane_off = (32*w) << 16`; `st_base[c] = OFF_SMEM_CD + 2048*w + 4*(l & 7) + 128*c + 32*(((l >> 3) & 3) ^ c)` for `c = 0..3` (byte offsets from `smem`, E.6.1 rule); `cd_base = smem_base + OFF_SMEM_CD`; `issue_store = (w == 0)`.
```
do {
  valid = clc_consume(tm_next, tn_next);                       // BEFORE storing the current tile (:878-886)
  st = accc_idx;  tok = mbar_try_wait(acc_full[st], accc_phase);   // one peek per tile (:813)
  taddr = T + tmem_lane_off + 128*st;
  #pragma unroll                                                // 8 subtiles fully unrolled (:818-820; DelayTmaStore = false for this config)
  for (e = 0; e < 8; ++e) {
    if (e == 0 && !tok) mbar_wait(acc_full[st], accc_phase);    // :864-867
    uint32_t r[16]; tmem_ld_32x32b_x16(taddr + 16*e, r);        // :881-883; no wait::ld for e < 7 (register scoreboards)
    if (e == 7) { tmem_wait_ld(); mbar_arrive_cluster_masked((acc_empty_addr(st)) & kPeerBitMask); advance<4>(accc_idx, accc_phase); }   // release before the arithmetic :886-890
    #pragma unroll
    for (j = 0; j < 16; ++j) { float t = alpha * __uint_as_float(r[j]); float d = fmaf(beta, 0.0f, t);
                               cd[(st_base[j & 3] + 512*(j >> 2) + 8192*(e & 3)) / 4] = d; }   // 16 scalar st.shared.b32; cd = (float*)(smem + 0)
    fence_proxy_async_shared_cta();  named_bar_sync(1, 128);
    if (issue_store) { #pragma unroll for (i = 0; i < 4; ++i) tma_store_3d(&params.tma_d, cd_base + 8192*(e & 3) + 2048*i, 128*tm + 32*i, 128*tn + 16*e, 0);
                       tma_store_commit_group(); tma_store_wait_read<1>(); }   // all 32 lanes of warp 4, uniform operands, no election (:740, :766-788)
    named_bar_sync(1, 128);
  }
  tm = tm_next; tn = tn_next;                                   // :932-933
} while (valid);                                                // no store_tail for beta = 0 (7.10, D15)
```
The smem stage index is the compile-time `e & 3` (8 subtiles cycle through 4 stages; B.3.3 step 17). The store's third coordinate is the literal 0 (`L = 1`). The C fragment is the literal `0.0f`; with run-time `beta` the front end cannot fold `fmaf(beta, 0.0f, t)` (beta may be Inf/NaN, and `t + (+0)` differs from `t` for `t = -0`), so `FMUL` + `FFMA` survive **[confirm 128 + 128 per tile in the first SASS dump]**; never `alpha * acc` alone, never a separate add.

**`clc_consume` (every participating warp, once per tile; 7.4, B.3.4 steps 5b-5c).** `mbar_wait(clc_full[clc_idx], clc_phase)`; `valid = clc_decode(response[clc_idx], x0, y0, z0)`; `fence_proxy_async_shared_cta()`; `mbar_arrive_remote(clc_empty[clc_idx], 0)` (`sm100_pipeline.hpp:1132-1135`); `advance<2>(clc_idx, clc_phase)`; `if (valid) { tm_next = 2*(y0 >> 1) + (x0 & 1) + x; tn_next = 2*(x0 >> 1) + (y0 & 1) + y; }` (general form of B.4's `(y0 + x, x0 + y)` for even `x0, y0`). Because the assignment is guarded, `tm_next`/`tn_next` are written only for a cancelled response; declare them once per role as `int tm_next = tm, tn_next = tn;` before the `do` loop (E.6.3 step 5 gives `tm`, `tn`) so that the unconditional `tm = tm_next; tn = tn_next;` at the end of each loop body (producer, scheduler, mma, epilogue) never reads an indeterminate value when `valid == 0` (the copied values are dead in that case, but must not be uninitialised); the equivalent alternative is to copy under `if (valid)` in all four loops. Do not instead drop the `if (valid)` guard inside `clc_consume`: E.6.7 forbids deriving anything from the id words of a not-cancelled response. Exactly 800 arrivals per response: 32 (rank-0 warp 1) + 4 CTAs x (32 + 32 + 128). A role that skips one consume deadlocks CTA 0's empty barrier.

### E.6.5 Pipeline state and tokens

Each state is `{uint32_t idx, phase}`; producers start `{0, 1}`, consumers `{0, 0}` (`make_producer_start_state`, `sm90_pipeline.hpp:254-260`); `template <uint32_t Stages> advance(idx, phase) { if (++idx == Stages) { idx = 0; phase ^= 1; } }` mirrors `PipelineState::operator++` (`:204-213`). States: mainloop producer (warp 2, 8), mainloop consumer (warp 0 leader, 8), accumulator producer (warp 0 both CTAs, 4), accumulator consumer (warps 4-7, 4), CLC producer (rank-0 warp 1, 2), CLC consumer (warps 0, 1 of rank 0, 2, 4-7; 2), throttle producer (rank-0 warp 2, 2), throttle consumer (rank-0 warp 1, 2). All are carried across tiles, never reset (128 % 16 == 0 makes every tile start the mainloop at `{0,1}`/`{0,0}`, but the code must not rely on it). Token: `tok` 1 = `WaitDone`, 0 = `WaitAgain`; `skip_wait` = `tok = 1` without a peek (`sm90_pipeline.hpp:590-597`). The tails continue the state (7.2), they do not apply one parity to all barriers.

### E.6.6 Register and instruction-shape discipline (D.2, D.7)

- Targets: `REG <= 68` (soft, see E.8), `STACK 0`, `LOCAL 0` under `__launch_bounds__(256,1)`. No run-time-indexed local arrays (`r[16]`, `st_base[4]` are indexed by unrolled constants only), no `__noinline__`, `__grid_constant__` params so `&params.tma_*` is a constant-bank address and no local copy is made.
- Uniform register file is the tight resource (D.7): in the MMA loop keep only `desc_lo_a`, `desc_lo_b`, `tmem_c`, `kInstrDesc`, `full_base`, `empty_base`, `cons_idx/phase`, `k`, `0xF` live; compute `lo + 1024*rs` per stage and `+2*kb` per k-block. The baseline's MMA k-tile loop is about 44 SASS instructions and the producer loop about 31; a materially larger loop means something above was violated.
- No lane-divergent work in warps 0-2 except the `elect_one_sync()` predicate (and `lane < 4` in W13); do not merge or split elections relative to the baseline's granularity: one `elect_one_sync()` per TMA A+B pair (`sm100_mma_warpspecialized.hpp:619-622`, `ptx.txt:889`/`:995`), one per `tcgen05.mma` (`mma_sm100_umma.hpp:563-586`), one per `tcgen05.commit` (`barrier.h:848-861`), one per `try_cancel` (E.6.2 W2); the PTX form is the contract, ptxas folds the elections into the uniform datapath (D.6 item 6).
- Loop shapes: `#pragma unroll 1` on both producer k-tile loops and the MMA k-tile loop (no peeled first iteration; `scale_c` handles the first MMA); full unroll of the 4 k-blocks, 8 subtiles, 16 registers, 4 store boxes; tails as unrolled `for` over compile-time `Stages`.
- `alpha`/`beta` read once at entry into two `float` locals used only in the epilogue; `k_tiles` may be held in a register (the baseline re-reads `K` per tile; either is fine, D.2).

### E.6.7 What not to do

No `cute::TiledMma`/`TiledCopy`/`make_tma_copy`/`Copy_Atom`/`cute::copy`/`cute::gemm`/`Tensor`/`Layout` objects, `PipelineState`, `Pipeline*`, `ClusterBarrier`, `NamedBarrier`, `TmemAllocator`, `PersistentTileSchedulerSm100`, collectives, `GemmUniversal*`, `FusionCallbacks` in the kernel TU (Section 1 item 3). No static `__shared__`, no `__align__` on the dynamic buffer, no `__noinline__`. No compile-time `alpha`/`beta`, no `alpha * acc` shortcut, no separate add. No per-thread `cp.async` for A/B, no `st.global` for D, no `tcgen05.fence`, no `__syncthreads()` in the roles. No election hoisted or split beyond the baseline's granularity (E.6.2 W2: one per TMA A+B pair, per MMA, per commit, per CLC query), no descriptor tables, no unrolled k-tile loops. No reading of `x0/y0/z0` from a not-cancelled response, no second `try_cancel` after one, no dependence on the hand-out order or on `N_native = 33`. No `wait_group.read 0` at exit: D.7 keeps the baseline's exit condition (at most one bulk group not awaited on its smem read, D15), and E.7.4/E.8 D3-D4 count `cp.async.bulk.wait_group.read 0` = 0 and `DEPBAR.LE SB0, 0x0` = 0 as hard, so an exit tightening is not an implementer option; the baseline's own `read 0` (`ptx.txt:4326`, SASS `0xb760`) belongs to the omitted `store_tail` C path (E.6.1 'Not needed') and is not reproduced.

## E.7 Bring-up, validation and diagnostics under the fixed commands

### E.7.1 Stage A: compile

- The only allowed edits are source edits, the toggle line, and the E.3.1 source-list addition. Expected: the new TU's flag line equals D.5 **[confirm in `flags.make`]**; `cuobjdump --dump-resource-usage` lists both kernels.
- Compile-time self-checks (zero run-time cost): `sizeof(ExplicitGemmParams)` and every `offsetof` used by the bridge asserted to the same numbers in both TUs; the Section 6.2 offsets, alignments and total (E.6.1); the descriptor constants; `alignof(CUtensorMap)`. A vacuous `static_assert` (one that only restates a definition) proves nothing; assert the *expected numbers*.
- Failure modes only visible here: inline-PTX operand/type errors are reported by `ptxas` quoting the PTX text, not a source line: keep every `asm` in its own small wrapper so the wrapper name identifies the site; `tcgen05.*`, `clusterlaunchcontrol.*`, `cta_group::2` TMA and `try_wait` with a suspend hint require `sm_100a` (a "requires .target sm_100a" error means the TU is compiled under a different arch guard); the 16-bit mask operands are `"h"` registers (passing 32-bit is an error; whether an immediate is accepted is untested); duplicate symbols at link time from a non-`inline` definition in a shared header (E.2.2, E.7.3); `-Xcompiler=-Wconversion` warnings are not errors (no `-Werror`, D.5).

### E.7.2 Stage B: correctness (the unchanged harness is the oracle)

Outcome signatures of the fixed run (match on message text, not line numbers, which the E.4 edit shifts):

| Outcome | Printed | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| Pass | `Disposition: Passed`, `Problem Size`, `Avg runtime`, `GFLOPS` | `D` of the warm-up launch is **value-equal** to the reference (`BlockCompareEqual` tests `a != b`, `tensor_compare.h:69`; `-0.0 == +0.0` passes, NaN fails, no location is printed); the ten timed launches are not checked |
| Launch rejected | `Got CUTLASS error while calling explicit_gemm::run(gemm) at line N: Error Internal` (or `Error Invalid Problem` / `Error Not Supported` from E.5.1) | the host function returned before/at the launch; most likely `cudaErrorInvalidValue` from a missing `MaxDynamicSharedMemorySize` opt-in, a grid not divisible by the cluster, or a wrong `numAttrs`. Produced by the checked return value of `cudaLaunchKernelExC` (E.5.2 item 6); item 7 does not change this row. A launch rejection that were somehow left unreported would show as `Disposition: Failed` (D never written) and, under the toggle, as a non-zero `TRACE_HOST cudaGetLastError`, not as the next row [inferred] |
| Device fault | `Got CUDA error while calling cudaDeviceSynchronize() at line N: <string>` | the kernel died: `illegal instruction` (malformed descriptor, `cta_group::2` from the wrong warp/CTA), `illegal memory access`/`misaligned address` (bad smem operand, bad tensormap address), `unspecified launch failure` (`__trap()`, including ptxas's TMEM dealloc consistency traps `BPT.TRAP`, D.2) **[strings inferred]**. Toggle on: the fault is first seen by the watchdog inside the warm-up `run()` (F.2 item 3), which prints the `TRACE_HANG*` records and `TRACE_HOST explicit_hang_records ... cuda_error <string>`; `run()` then returns `kErrorInternal` and the harness prints the E.4 `Got CUTLASS error while calling explicit_gemm::run(gemm)` line instead of this row's `cudaDeviceSynchronize()` message |
| Wrong result | `Disposition: Failed`, exit 255 | kernel completed, some element differs; no location |
| Hang | nothing printed (the example prints nothing before `Disposition`), process alive at full utilisation | a warp waits forever; the host is inside `cudaDeviceSynchronize()` in `verify()` |
| Passed, then hang or error at the timed loop | `Disposition: Passed` then silence or `Got CUTLASS error ... at line N` | cross-launch leak (TMEM not deallocated so the next `tcgen05.alloc` blocks, **inferred**) or an exit race that only bites when the next kernel lands |

Toggle-on diagnostics (C.1 discipline: guarded blocks, records not text, no added barriers or elections; all in the kernel TU):

- **B1 bounded blocking wait.** The one blocking-wait helper reads `%globaltimer` on entry and after each failed `try_wait`; after 4 s it writes a `HangRec {site_id, bx, by, rank, warp, lane, barrier_addr, parity, tile_counter, k_tile, time}` into a host-mapped pinned buffer (`cudaHostAlloc(..., cudaHostAllocMapped)`; device pointer as a toggle-only params field; slot `((by*64 + bx)*8 + warp)`, 2 MiB), `__threadfence_system()`, spins one more second so every stuck warp records, then `__trap()`. The host (toggle on) synchronises after the warm-up and prints `TRACE_HANG site=<name> bx by rank warp lane addr off parity t q`; `off` maps to the 6.2 names, `parity`/`t`/`q` to the 7.2 formulas. Site ids: `WAIT_MAINLOOP_FULL/EMPTY`, `WAIT_ACC_FULL/EMPTY`, `WAIT_CLC_FULL/EMPTY`, `WAIT_THROTTLE_FULL/EMPTY`, `WAIT_TMEM_DEALLOC`, `TAIL_MAINLOOP/ACC/CLC`. Toggle off: the bare baseline loop **[pinned-buffer visibility after `__trap()` inferred]**.
- **B2 heartbeat + watchdog** for non-mbarrier hangs (`barrier.cluster.wait`, `bar.sync` population mismatch, blocked `tcgen05.alloc`, `wait_group.read`): lane 0 of each role warp writes `{tile_counter, phase_marker}` to its pinned slot at each tile start and tail step; a host `std::thread` polls `cudaEventQuery` on an event recorded after the warm-up launch and after 20 s prints every slot (`TRACE_HANG_HB ...`) and `std::_Exit(3)`. This replaces the forbidden `timeout` wrapper **[legality of `cudaEventQuery` from a second thread inferred]**.
- **B3 sentinel fill of D**: `cudaMemset(block_D, 0xFF)` before the warm-up so never-stored tiles read as NaN instead of stale `cudaMalloc` contents (`device_memory.h:61` does not clear). The toggle-off `Passed` is therefore evidence of arithmetic, not of coverage; coverage is established by the toggle-on run (sentinel + K4b/K5).
- **B4 mismatch dump** (after `verify()` when `!passed`): copy `D` and `ref_D` to the host and print total mismatches; a 64x64 tile map (`.` clean, `#` all wrong, `x` partial, `N` sentinel); for the first bad tile the fraction per 64-row half, 32-row quarter (= epilogue warp), 16-column subtile and `(col % 4, row % 32)` class; whether the tile equals the reference of another tile (`(tm,tn) holds (tm',tn')`); the sign-of-zero disagreements (compare bit patterns, the harness does not); a histogram of `D/ref` (a constant `r/128` means `r` k-tiles were accumulated). D is column-major (`n*M + m`) and every value is an exact integer (Section 8), so all classifications are exact.
- **B5 order of the first runs.** Build toggle **on** first and run once (hang bound, sentinel, dump, records, checker in one go); if `Passed` and `check_trace.py` reports 0 FAIL, build toggle **off** and run (the trace build has different register pressure, 108 vs 68 in the baseline, C.8; a race can hide in either; both must pass). The toggle-off binary is the one for E.7.4 and E.8. `restore` is redefined for the new work: two toggle-off builds of the *new* source must give identical SASS of the explicit kernel (the script's current `restore` compares against the baseline pristine and would always report a difference).

Failure-mode table (symptom -> most likely cause -> cheapest probe): mainloop deadlock at `q = 0` -> `expect_tx != 49152`, non-leader arming, unmasked TMA barrier operand (peer's boxes credit the peer), wrong A mask (`0x5 << x`) -> hang record + K4a; at `q = 8/16` -> empty count/commit mask/parity flip -> hang record; accumulator hang at `t = 4` -> unmasked peer release (leader sees 128 of 256) or wrong commit mask -> hang record + K1 `peer_mask_maps_odd_to_even`; CLC hang at `t = 0` on one CTA -> that CTA not armed by lane `rank`; stall after 2 tiles -> a role consuming without releasing or empty count != 800 -> K5a/K5b; whole tiles wrong and `(tm,tn) holds (tm',tn')` -> transpose/intra-cluster offset/response-word mix-up -> B4 + K5c; 64-row halves -> A coordinate `64*y` or destination `8192*y`; 64-column halves -> B coordinate `64*x`; rows mod 32 -> TMEM lane term; columns with a mod-4 pattern -> the `STS` swizzle or store source `2048*i`; 16-column groups swapped -> `16*e` in the `tcgen05.ld` address vs the store coordinate; ratio `r/128` -> stage/phase skip or `scale_c` not cleared per tile; only sign-of-zero differences -> `fmaf` dropped; `N` tiles -> never stored or stored elsewhere; silent garbage / `illegal instruction` -> descriptor fields (`layout_type 2`, `SBO 64`, `LBO 1`, 14-bit start) -> K3; TMEM: hang at start -> alloc not from a full warp 0 in both CTAs or `bar.arrive 6,160` missing; `unspecified launch failure` at the end -> dealloc base/count mismatch or dealloc before the peer finished; `Passed` then hang in iteration 2 -> relinquish/dealloc missing; intermittent faults -> a dropped tail (K_TAIL records, PTX tail inventory, five toggle-off runs). Base/alignment: K1 FAIL or `SHARED != 1024` -> static `__shared__` crept in (also from toggle code).

### E.7.3 Reusing the Part C trace infrastructure

- **Unchanged**: `deabstraction_trace.hpp` record format and macros; `check_trace.py` (the working-tree version with the host-adapter macro as INFO must be kept or committed, otherwise "0 FAIL" is unattainable: the committed version reports `4105 PASS, 1 FAIL`, `trace_out/checklist.txt`); the H2b block in `copy_traits_sm90_tma.hpp` (the `TRACE_ENCODE` lines still appear because `gemm.initialize()` still encodes six maps); `trace_host_probes<Gemm>(gemm)` (H1 offsets of the CUTLASS `SharedStorage` = the 6.2 reference; H2 `TRACE_TMAP` words = exactly the maps the explicit kernel receives; H4; H5); the K0 kernel.
- **Changed**: `trace_host_post_run` (`deabstraction_trace_probes.hpp:300-356`, working tree: `cudaGetLastError` and kernel attributes `:304-318`, occupancy `:319-335`, device attributes `:336-354`, `post_run_done` `:355`; all three blocks move with the function, since `check_trace.py` reads the `device_*` lines) takes `void const* fn` = the explicit kernel's address so that `kernel_maxDynamicSharedSizeBytes 230400`, `kernel_sharedSizeBytes 0`, `kernel_nonPortableClusterSizeAllowed 1`, `max_active_clusters` (expect 33) describe the explicit kernel; the K1-K5 device probes are re-inserted in the explicit kernel with the same kinds and payload meaning (K1 after `cluster_wait`, thread 0 of cluster (0,0): base and the 6.2 offsets as computed by the kernel's own arithmetic, including the reserved ones; K2 warp 0 after reading `T` and epilogue after `bar.sync 6,160`; K3 before each `tcgen05.mma` inside the elected region; K4a before the A/B TMA with the **unmasked** local `full[s]` address; K4b before each of the four stores with `__popc(__activemask())`; K5a before `try_cancel`; K5b/K5c in `clc_consume` after the mapping). Additive: `K_TAIL` (kind 13; kinds 12-15 are free, `K_COUNT = 16`), one record per completed tail **step**, written by lane 0 of the tail's warp after the step's wait returns, with the site id of that wait (`TAIL_MAINLOOP` per mainloop empty wait, `TAIL_CLC` per CLC slot, `TAIL_ACC` per accumulator stage, `WAIT_TMEM_DEALLOC` for the dealloc handshake wait; all four are in the B1 list) and the pipeline `{idx, phase}` that the wait used (recorded after the wait returns and before `++state`; for the mainloop tail every record therefore shows parity 1, Section 7.5); expected per rank: rank 0 = 8 + 2 + 4 + 1 = 15, rank 1 = 8 + 1 = 9, rank 2 = 8 + 4 + 1 = 13, rank 3 = 8 + 1 = 9 (46 per cluster; the accumulator tail exists only in the two leaders, the CLC tail only in rank 0). `check_trace.py` counts records per (rank, site id) against these numbers, not per warp. A discriminator (a K1 payload field the CUTLASS blocks never emit, plus `TRACE_HOST explicit_kernel_name`) proves the records come from the explicit kernel and not from the CUTLASS one still in the binary.
- **Two-TU linkage (toggle-on build only)** **[inferred; confirm at first toggle-on build]**: the five `__device__` globals of `deabstraction_trace.hpp:69-73` become `static __device__`, `trace_reset/dump/disable` and `trace_probe_kernel_k0` `static inline`, the two probe kernels `static __global__` (the `CUTLASS_GLOBAL` form), `trace_host_post_run` a non-template `static inline` taking `void const*`; all recording, K0 and the dump execute in the kernel TU through `explicit_trace_begin()`/`explicit_trace_end("launch0.csv")`, whose buffer is the one the explicit kernel writes; `g_trace_encode_count` stays `inline int`. Include order in the kernel TU: util header -> guarded `deabstraction_trace.hpp` -> `cute/swizzle.hpp` -> kernel. Under the toggle the kernel TU includes `deabstraction_trace_probes.hpp` (which pulls in CUTLASS headers for `Allocator2Sm`/`IsGdcGloballyEnabled`); this is toggle-only and must be said in the report.
- **Run script**: `EX` points at the util header; `PRISTINE`/`OUT` must not be overwritten (`step_build` copies `$BIN` to `$PRISTINE`; use `TRACE_OUT=trace_out_v2` for the new work and keep `trace_out/70_blackwell_fp16_gemm.pristine` for E.8); `step_inspect` runs on `$BIN` with a `KNAME` substring and the E.7.4 regexes; a `check_sass.py` evaluates the E.7.4 rows on the new dumps and on `trace_out/ptx.txt:45-4343` / `sass_gemm.txt` side by side. `check_trace.py` additions: parse `TRACE_HANG*` as FAIL, count `K_TAIL` per rank, `sizeof_ExplicitGemmParams`, INFO lines for the explicit kernel's attributes.

### E.7.4 Stage D: structural equivalence (toggle-off binary; no GPU needed)

- **Extraction**: `cuobjdump --dump-resource-usage`, `--dump-sass`, `--dump-ptx`, `--list-elf` on the binary; cut the explicit kernel with `awk '/^Fatbin /{p=0} /Function :/{p=index($0, pat) > 0} p'` (SASS; the `Fatbin` reset stops the cut at the next cubin so that a trailing kernel of another architecture is not appended) and by `.visible .entry`/`.entry` with the same reset (PTX). Two `sm_100a` cubins are expected (E.3.2). Baseline reference counts come from `trace_out/ptx.txt:45-4343` (the GEMM `.entry`; the file also holds four reference kernels) and `trace_out/sass_gemm.txt`. Regexes: count `mbarrier\.init` with a literal dot (a bare `.` also matches `fence.mbarrier_init`), exclude `PREEXIT` when counting `EXIT`, exclude `UTMASTG` when counting `STG`; the values quoted in the PTX row for `expect_tx` (49152, 16) and `bar.sync`/`bar.arrive` (1,128 / 6,160) are `mov.b32`-materialised register operands (7.2) and are verified through the feeding `mov.b32` or the SASS immediates (D.7), never by a literal match on the instruction line; only `cp.async.bulk.wait_group.read 1/0`, `shfl.sync.idx.b32 ..., 0, 31, -1`, `bar.warp.sync -1` and `and.b32 ..., -16777224` carry matchable immediates.
- **Resources** (`resources.txt:30` baseline `REG:68 STACK:0 SHARED:1024 LOCAL:0 CONSTANT[0]:2944`): hard `LOCAL:0`, `STACK:0`, `SHARED:1024` (system-reserved; anything larger means static `__shared__`); soft `REG <= 68` (E.8); `CONSTANT[0]` = 0x380 + `sizeof(ExplicitGemmParams)` (informational).
- **PTX inventory (exact contract)**: `tcgen05.mma.cta_group::2.kind::f16` 4; `tcgen05.commit ... multicast::cluster` 2; `tcgen05.ld ... 32x32b.x16` 8; `tcgen05.wait::ld` 1; `alloc`/`dealloc`/`relinquish_alloc_permit` 1 each; `cp.async.bulk.tensor.3d ... cta_group::2` 4 (two producer loops); store `...global.shared::cta.bulk_group` 32; `commit_group` 8; `wait_group.read 1` 8 (`read 0` 0); `try_cancel` 1; `is_canceled`/`get_first_ctaid` one per consume site; `griddepcontrol.wait` 2, `launch_dependents` 1; `prefetch.tensormap` 4; `mbarrier.init` 33 (43 variant: 43); `mbarrier.arrive.expect_tx.shared::cta` 2 sites (one per producer loop; the 49152 is a `mov.b32`-fed register operand, 7.2, so verify it from the feeding `mov.b32 %r, 49152` or from the SASS immediate, D.7, not by a literal regex); `mbarrier.arrive.expect_tx.shared::cluster` 1 (the 16 likewise); `try_wait.parity` one site per protocol step; `test_wait.parity` 2 (or 0 with a blocking CLC tail); `mapa.shared::cluster` = consume sites + 4 (one per W10/W13 site: each inlined `clc_consume` copy, the throttle release, the two dealloc-handshake arrivals, the CLC arming; 8 with the four `clc_consume` call sites of E.6.4; soft; baseline 26, `ptx.txt`, D.2, of which 17 belong to the omitted C path, 8 `epi_load` full arrivals and 9 `epi_load` empty releases, and 9 are comparable: 5 CLC releases, 1 throttle, 2 handshake, 1 arming); `mul.f32` 128, `fma.rn.f32` 128, `add.f32` 0; `st.shared.b32` 128 (not generic `st.b32`); `ld.shared.b128` = consume sites; `fence.proxy.async.shared::cta` 8 + consume sites; `fence.mbarrier_init.release.cluster` 4 (43 variant: 6); `barrier.cluster.arrive.relaxed.aligned`/`wait.aligned` 1 each; `bar.sync` 17 sites and `bar.arrive` 1 (ids and thread counts are `mov.b32`-fed register operands, `bar.sync %rA, %rB;`, 7.2: 16 `bar.sync` fed by (1, 128), 1 by (6, 160), the `bar.arrive` by (6, 160); a literal regex such as `bar.sync 1, 128` matches nothing in `ptx.txt`; the immediates are checked in the SASS row below); `elect.sync` >= 9; `and.b32 ..., -16777224` present; masks as `.b16`. Absent: `cp.async.ca/cg`, `wgmma`, `st.global`, `atom`, `trap` (toggle off), `.local`, `ld.global`, `ld.param` of `m/n/l` (only `k`, `alpha`, `beta` and the map addresses are read).
- **SASS executed-path inventory** (D.7 list): hard `UTCHMMA.2CTA` 4, `UTCBAR.2CTA.MULTICAST` 2, `UTMASTG.3D` 32, `UTMACMDFLUSH` 8, `DEPBAR.LE SB0, 0x1` 8 (`0x0` 0), `LDTM.x16` 8, `UGETNEXTWORKID.BROADCAST` 1, `PREEXIT` 1, `FFMA` 128, `FADD` 0, `LDL`/`STL` 0; soft `UTMALDG.3D.MULTICAST.2CTA` + `UTMALDG.3D.2CTA` 4 (no plain `UTMALDG.3D`), `UTMACCTL.PF` 4, `ACQBULK` 2, `FMUL` 128 + O(2), `STS` 128 + O(3), `SYNCS.EXCH.64` 33, `BAR.SYNC.DEFER_BLOCKING 0x1, 0x80` 16 / `0x6, 0xa0` 1 / `BAR.ARV 0x6, 0xa0` 1, `0xfefffff8` present, `UPRMT ... 0x5410` >= 1, `BPT.TRAP` 2, `MOV.SPILL`/`R2UR.FILL` informational (baseline 44 each, none in the hot loops). Hot-loop shape (soft): MMA loop ~44 instructions with four `UTCHMMA`, one `UTCBAR`, no `ELECT`; producer loop ~31 with two `UTMALDG`; subtile order `LDTM -> {16 FMUL, 16 FFMA, 16 STS} -> MEMBAR.ALL.CTA; FENCE.VIEW.ASYNC.S -> BAR.SYNC -> 4 UTMASTG -> UTMACMDFLUSH -> DEPBAR.LE SB0, 0x1 -> BAR.SYNC` (the counts are exact; the FMUL/FFMA/STS ordering within the braces is ptxas's and not a target: the baseline emits 4 FMUL, 4 FFMA, then rounds of 3 FMUL / 3 FFMA with single STS interleaved, then the last 8 STS contiguously, identically in all eight subtiles, `trace_out/sass_gemm.txt` 0x7c10-0x7f00; only the opcodes from `MEMBAR.ALL.CTA` onward must appear in the stated order); the release `SYNCS.ARRIVE.TRANS64.RED.A1T0 [.. & 0xfefffff8]` before subtile 7's arithmetic. Deviations are explained, not necessarily fixed, unless E.8's timing gate fails. The CUTLASS kernel's SASS in the same binary must equal `trace_out/sass_gemm.txt`.

## E.8 Acceptance criteria for the final report

| # | Criterion | Hard / soft | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | Fixed build succeeds with only the E.3.1 source-list addition; the new TU's flags equal D.5 | hard | build log, `flags.make` |
| A2 | All `static_assert`s of E.6.1/E.7.1 compile | hard | build |
| B1 | `Disposition: Passed`, toggle off, 5 of 5 runs | hard | run log |
| B2 | `Disposition: Passed`, toggle on, `check_trace.py` 0 FAIL including `K_TAIL` and the discriminator | hard | `host.txt`, `launch0.csv`, `checklist.txt` |
| B3 | No `TRACE_HANG`/`TRACE_HANG_HB` line in the toggle-on run | hard | `host.txt` |
| C1 | SM clock 1965 MHz in every steady-state sample (inferred for the ~9 ms loops themselves, as in D.4); `Passed` in all timed runs | hard | `clocks.csv` |
| C2 | Median `Avg runtime` of 5 toggle-off runs of the replacement, interleaved with 5 runs of the baseline binary (B R B R ...). The reference is the median of the 5 same-day baseline runs, not the 2026-09-09 value (C3 only gates that the machine is in the same state) | tiered | replacement median <= same-day baseline median x 1.002 accept; x 1.002 .. x 1.005 explain; > x 1.005 fail. Report both medians and the ratio. For a same-day baseline median equal to the 2026-09-09 value 0.888659 ms the absolute thresholds are 0.890436 and 0.893102 ms |
| C3 | Same-day baseline median within 0.3% of 0.888659 ms | gate | else re-baseline and say so. The baseline binary is `trace_out/70_blackwell_fp16_gemm.pristine`: the toggle-off build of the 3709e725 tree, SASS-identical to the recorded baseline but not byte-reproducible (D.0), built with `CUDA_RUNTIME_LIBRARY Shared`; it runs only with the same toolkit path present **[confirm]**; record `ldd` of both binaries |
| C4 | Per-iteration host sequence stated: `initialize()` unchanged (6 encodes + 1 attribute call on the CUTLASS kernel) + 2 attribute calls on the explicit kernel + 1 launch + `cudaGetLastError`; nothing synchronises | hard (report) | code review, optional `nsys` API trace |
| D1 | `LOCAL:0 STACK:0 SHARED:1024` | hard | `cuobjdump --dump-resource-usage` |
| D2 | `REG <= 68` | soft, disclosed | D.7 states 68 (the baseline's `resources.txt:30` value) as a target, not a bound. The kernel runs at 1 CTA/SM regardless of registers (230400 B dynamic smem, Section 6.2), so any `REG <= 255` (the `__launch_bounds__(256,1)` cap) with `LOCAL:0` has no occupancy effect (E.5.4: `N_native = 33` for any `REG <= 255`) and is reportable as a deviation for the user to accept, not silently; a register demand ptxas cannot fit under the cap appears as spills and fails D1 (`LOCAL:0`), not D2. A count well above 68 is a symptom of extra live state (E.9 risk 2) and is the first thing to examine if C2 fails |
| D3 | PTX inventory of E.7.4 | hard for tensor-core/TMA/CLC/`griddepcontrol`/arithmetic/`bar` counts and the absence list; soft for `elect.sync`, `mapa`, `try_wait` site counts | `ptx_new.txt` |
| D4 | SASS executed-path inventory of E.7.4 | hard/soft as listed | `sass_explicit.txt` |
| D5 | Hot-loop shape | soft | SASS reading |
| D6 | Two toggle-off builds give identical SASS for the explicit kernel | hard | SASS diff (binary `cmp` is known not to reproduce, D.0) |
| E1 | No hidden layers (Section 1 item 3); tensor maps taken from `gemm.params()`; harness diff = E.4; CMake diff = E.3.1 | hard | code review, `git diff` |

## E.9 Risk register

| # | Risk | P | Impact | Mitigation / detection |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Dynamic smem base not `0x400` (static `__shared__` appears, e.g. from toggle code) | low | high: SW128 stages lose 1 KiB alignment, descriptor field may overflow, every 6.2 offset shifts | no static smem in the kernel TU (trace state in global/pinned memory); compute addresses from the run-time base and only check `0x400`; K1; `SHARED:1024`; H3 `sharedSizeBytes 0` |
| 2 | Register count above 68 | medium | low-medium (no occupancy effect; symptom of extra live state) | `__launch_bounds__(256,1)`, E.6.6 discipline; `cuobjdump`; D2 disclosed |
| 3 | Compiler folds or reshapes the epilogue arithmetic | low with `fmaf` and run-time alpha/beta | medium (structural; a `Failed` is unlikely on integer data) | E.6.4 form; `mul.f32`/`fma.rn.f32`/`add.f32` counts 128/128/0 |
| 4 | ptxas reshapes details (retry copies, `ELECT` folding, `UMOV` rematerialisation, `PREEXIT` position) | high | low: inventory and runtime are the targets, not addresses | compare PTX exactly, SASS by inventory; investigate only if C2 fails |
| 5 | Attribute-call count 3 vs 2 per iteration | certain | very low (host-only, hidden except once) | state it (C4); optional `nsys` |
| 6 | CUTLASS kernel stays in the binary and keeps receiving `initialize()`'s attribute call | certain | none for correctness or timing parity; larger fatbin; one more module load before the timer | E.3.3; SASS equality check of the CUTLASS kernel |
| 7 | Timing noise / machine state | medium | medium: a 0.2% band needs a stable machine | five interleaved runs each, clock log, same-day baseline gate (C3) |
| 8 | Toggle-on build fails to link (two TUs, header-defined `__device__` globals and `__global__` probe kernels) | high unless E.7.3 is applied | blocks the trace stage only | E.7.3 `static` changes; recording in the kernel TU |
| 9 | Toggle-only code changes the performance binary | low | medium | everything under the toggle; D6 SASS identity of two toggle-off builds |
| 10 | Harness blind spots: duplicated tiles, exit races, coverage | certain | high if unchecked | K5 exactly-once, `K_TAIL`, PTX tail inventory, sentinel fill, five toggle-off runs |
| 11 | First run hangs with no diagnostics | medium | lost time | B5 order: toggle on first |
| 12 | `N_native`/occupancy differs for the new kernel | low | none for correctness (D.7); would change per-cluster tile counts | H3 `max_active_clusters` on the explicit kernel (expect 33) |
| 13 | Toolchain/driver differs from D.5 on the day | low | medium | `config.txt` vs `trace_out/config.txt`; same-day baseline run |
| 14 | `griddepcontrol` dropped or added | low | none at run time (no PDL); changes the inventory | keep 2 `wait` + 1 `launch_dependents` |
| 15 | `alignof(CUtensorMap)` is 128 in the CUDA 13 headers | possible | none if `ExplicitGemmParams` starts with the maps and the `static_assert` reads `>= 64`; a `== 64` assert would fail | E.2.2 assert form; `sizeof` check |
| 16 | ctest entries of the target now fail (unsupported swizzle/shape) | certain if ctest is run | none for the fixed commands | E.3.4; loud `kErrorInvalidProblem`; user informed |
| 17 | `run_script step_build` overwrites the committed pristine binary | certain if run unchanged | loses the E.8 baseline binary | `TRACE_OUT=trace_out_v2`; never run `step_build` on the old `OUT` |

## E.10 Decisions left to the user (defaults in bold; the implementation of 2026-09-11 (Part F) uses every default below)

1. File names: **`70_blackwell_fp16_gemm_explicit.cu/.hpp/_util.hpp`** in `examples/70_blackwell_gemm/`.
2. The one-line CMake source-list addition (E.3.1): **accepted** (the alternative, header-only option C, violates the "all device code in the `.cu`" requirement).
3. `MaxDynamicSharedMemorySize` on the explicit kernel: **set on every call** (parity of attribute calls on the launched kernel) rather than once behind a static flag.
4. Guard policy: **fail loudly with `kErrorInvalidProblem`/`kErrorNotSupported`** for arguments outside Section 0 (three of the four registered ctest entries then fail; the fixed commands are unaffected).
5. `beta != 0`: **rejected by the guard** (the C path is omitted). Alternative: run anyway and document that `D` is wrong for `beta != 0`.
6. Barrier initialisation: **33 inits and 4 init fences** (omit the `epi_load` and `load_order` barriers of the C path; the CLC empty count is the constant 800). Alternative: 43 + 6 for count parity with the baseline.
7. `tma_c` carried in the parameter struct and prefetched: **yes** (4 `UTMACCTL.PF` parity; never used for the run). Alternative: drop it (3 prefetches, smaller struct).
8. C fragment: **literal `0.0f`** (frees 16 registers relative to the baseline's zero-filled fragment; identical arithmetic).
9. Template bridge in the header (`make_params`, `run(Gemm const&)`): **accepted** (the only way to keep the harness edit at the four lines of E.4, toggle line, one include and two call sites, without naming the CUTLASS `Params` type in the harness).
10. Option B (the host function performs the six encodes itself and `gemm.initialize()` leaves the timed loop): **deferred** to a later phase after E passes.
11. `REG` criterion: **soft** (E.8 D2), disclosed as a deviation from D.7's "at most 68" if exceeded.

# Part F. Implementation record (2026-09-11; written against Parts A-E; not compiled on this host)

Part F records what was actually written, where the code departs from the letter of Part E and why, what the review passes over the code found, and what only the B200 can settle. The code is the deliverable; this part is the map from the note to the code and the checklist for the first build and run. Nothing in Part F was compiled or executed: this host has no `nvcc`, no CUDA headers and no GPU.

## F.1 What was written

| File (in `examples/70_blackwell_gemm/`) | Lines | Content |

Anonymous namespace inside `explicit_gemm`: the E.6.1 constants and Section 6.2 offsets with `static_assert`s (including the D.7 descriptor words and the `cute::Swizzle<2,5,2>` cross-check of the epilogue formula), the trace-only `HangRec`/`WaitSite` definitions and the `EXPLICIT_WAIT` macro, the 37 PTX wrappers of E.6.2 (each `asm` copied from the CUTLASS source named in its comment), `advance<Stages>`, `desc64`, `clc_consume<TraceKind>`, `load_ktiles`, `producer_role`, `scheduler_role`, `mma_role`, `epilogue_role`. Then the one `__global__` kernel `explicit_blackwell_fp16_gemm_kernel(const __grid_constant__ ExplicitGemmParams)` with `__launch_bounds__(256, 1)`, `extern __shared__ char smem[]` (no alignment attribute, no static shared memory) and the E.6.3 prologue + role dispatch; the host function `explicit_gemm::run(ExplicitGemmParams const&)` (E.5.1 guards, then the E.5.2 mirror of `run()`); under the toggle the host entry points `trace_begin`, `trace_end`, the hang/heartbeat report and the B4 mismatch dump |

Toggle-guarded blocks re-pointed: `explicit_gemm::trace_begin(block_D.get(), bytes)` where `trace_reset(); trace_probe_kernel_k0();` was, and `explicit_gemm::trace_end("launch0.csv", result.passed, block_D.get(), block_ref_D.get(), options.m, options.n)` where `trace_host_post_run<Gemm>(); trace_dump(...); trace_disable();` was; `trace_host_probes<Gemm>(gemm)` unchanged |

| `check_sass.py` (new) | ~280 | The E.7.4 rows: PTX inventory (hard/soft), the absence list, the `mov.b32`-materialised operand values, the SASS executed-path inventory, hot-loop shape hints, resources (`LOCAL 0`, `STACK 0`, `SHARED 1024` hard; `REG <= 68` soft); optional side-by-side baseline counts. Dry run on the baseline dumps (the GEMM `.entry` cut out of `trace_out/ptx.txt` first with the E.7.4 awk, since that file holds five kernels; `trace_out/sass_gemm.txt`, `trace_out/resources.txt`): 82 PASS / 7 FAIL / 4 WARN. The FAILs are the rows where the CUTLASS kernel legitimately differs from the explicit contract: C-path `cp.async.bulk.tensor` loads 32, `wait_group.read 0` 1 (the store tail), `griddepcontrol.wait` 3 (the epilogue-load warp's), `mbarrier.init` 43 and `fence.mbarrier_init` 6 (the 43-init variant), `ld.global` 5 (the pointer-form scalars), `DEPBAR.LE SB0, 0x0` 1. The WARNs: `mov.b32 ..., 800` 0 (the baseline selects the CLC count at run time, `selp.b32 %r, 928, 800, %p`), plain `UTMALDG.3D` 32 (C loads), `ACQBULK` 3, `SYNCS.EXCH.64` 43. On the explicit kernel's dumps every hard row must PASS [confirm at first build] |

Kernel structure in one paragraph (the code follows E.6.3-E.6.5 line for line): every thread computes `smem_base = __cvta_generic_to_shared(smem)`, `warp_idx` (shfl), `lane_pred` (`elect.sync`), `rank`, `is_leader = !(rank & 1)`, `peer = rank ^ 1`, `is_cta0`, `alpha`, `beta`, `k_tiles = (k + 63) >> 6`; warp 1's and warp 3's elected lanes prefetch A/B and C/D; the 33 `mbarrier.init`s are issued by the elected lane of warps 0 (mainloop 1/2), 4 (CLC 1/800), 5 (accumulator 1/256), 3 (throttle 32/32) with a `fence.mbarrier_init.release.cluster` by all threads after each group, then warp 0's `lane_pred` lane initialises `tmem_dealloc` (32); `barrier.cluster.arrive.relaxed.aligned`; `(x, y) = %cluster_ctaid`; `tm = 2*(blockIdx.y >> 1) + (blockIdx.x & 1)`, `tn = 2*(blockIdx.x >> 1) + (blockIdx.y & 1)`; `barrier.cluster.wait.aligned`; dispatch `warp 2 -> producer_role`, `warp 1 && is_cta0 -> scheduler_role`, `warp 0 -> mma_role`, `warp >= 4 -> epilogue_role`, the rest exit. Pipeline states are `{idx, phase}` pairs (producers `{0,1}`, consumers `{0,0}`, `advance<Stages>`); every blocking wait is `EXPLICIT_WAIT(addr, parity, site, tile, q)`, which is the baseline's `mbarrier.try_wait.parity ... 0x989680` loop with the toggle off and the bounded, hang-recording variant with the toggle on; every peek is `mbar_try_wait`.

## F.2 Deviations from the letter of Part E, and why

1. **Toggle-on entry points**: named `explicit_gemm::trace_begin(void* d, size_t bytes)` and `explicit_gemm::trace_end(csv, passed, d, ref_d, m, n)` (E.7.3 called them `explicit_trace_begin()/explicit_trace_end(path)`), declared in the `.hpp` under the toggle; `trace_begin` also performs the B3 sentinel fill (`cudaMemset(D, 0xFF)`) and `trace_end` the B4 mismatch dump, so the harness keeps exactly the two guarded call sites it had (plus the unchanged `trace_host_probes<Gemm>`).
2. **Hang-record buffer pointer** reaches the kernel through a `static __device__ HangRec* g_explicit_hang_buf` set by `cudaMemcpyToSymbol` in `trace_begin`, not through a toggle-only field of `ExplicitGemmParams` (E.7.2 B1), so the parameter block is identical in both builds. Slot `((by*64 + bx)*8 + warp)`, 64 bytes each, 2 MiB pinned (`cudaHostAllocMapped`).
3. **Watchdog (B2)** polls `cudaEventQuery` on the main thread (10 ms sleep, 20 s bound) instead of a second `std::thread`; on a fault it prints the `TRACE_HANG` records (the first 64; the total is in the `TRACE_HOST explicit_hang_records` line) and every heartbeat slot (`TRACE_HANG_HB`, uncapped), on a timeout it prints them and calls `std::_Exit(3)`. Consequence: with the toggle on, a device fault of the warm-up launch is reported by `run()` (return `kErrorInternal`, harness line `Got CUTLASS error while calling explicit_gemm::run(gemm)`), not by the `cudaDeviceSynchronize()` of `verify()` as the E.7.2 'Device fault' row describes for the toggle-off binary. It runs only for the warm-up launch (`g_explicit_trace_armed`, set by `trace_begin`, cleared by `trace_end`), inside `run()` after `cudaGetLastError`; the toggle-on `run()` therefore synchronises once, which C.8 already excludes from any timing.
4. **`K_TAIL` payload**: `{site, idx, phase, tile}` where `idx/phase` are the values the tail step's wait used (recorded after the wait returns and before `++state`). E.7.3 was amended in place in the working tree to say so (HEAD 0ddb03fc read "the pipeline `{idx, phase}` after the step"); `check_trace.py` is written to the recorded semantics (mainloop tail parity 1 for every step).
5. **`trace_host_post_run`**: replaced by `trace_host_post_run_fn(void const* fn)` (`static inline`), called by `trace_end` with the explicit kernel's address; the old template was removed (an external-linkage template naming a TU-local static).
6. **Epilogue smem stores** are C++ stores through `uint32_t*` (`__float_as_uint(d)`) into `smem + offset`, the generic pointer of the `extern __shared__` array, so that nvcc emits `st.shared.b32` as the baseline's `uint32_t` recast copy does (the `float*` form of E.6.1 would emit `st.shared.f32`); the `st.shared.b32` asm fallback of E.6.1 was not needed [confirm at first build: `st.shared.b32` 128 in `ptx_explicit.txt`].
7. **`T` (TMEM base) reads** are plain `uint32_t` loads (as the source), not `volatile`: their order after `tcgen05.alloc` is guaranteed by the `"memory"` clobber of the intervening `bar.arrive 6,160` / `bar.sync 6,160`.
8. **`clc_consume`** takes a trace-kind template parameter (`K_CLC_SCHED`, `K_CLC_MMA`, or 0) and a trace-only consume counter so that K5b/K5c records carry `state.count` as `check_trace.py` expects; with the toggle off the counter is dead and the parameter is 0.
9. **Run script**: `OUT` defaults to `trace_out_v2` and the new binary is saved as `70_blackwell_fp16_gemm.explicit` (E.9 item 17); `inspect`/`restore` operate on the explicit kernel by name; `baseline` interleaves with the pristine binary (E.8 C2-C3).
10. **The util header** includes `<cstdint>` (for the `uint32_t`/`uint64_t` descriptor constants); E.2.3 was amended in place in the working tree to list it (HEAD 0ddb03fc listed only `<cuda_runtime.h>`, `<cstdio>` and `cutlass/cutlass.h`).
11. **`status_from_cuda`** returns `kInvalid` on any CUDA error (the `Return_Status` value); `run()` collapses `kInvalid` and a non-success `cudaGetLastError` to `kErrorInternal` exactly as the adapter does.
12. **E.2.3 helpers not written**: there is no `EXPLICIT_GEMM_RETURN_IF_ERROR(expr)` macro (E.2.3, E.5.2 item 7) and no `explicit_gemm::trace_printf(...)`; `run()` carries a `cutlass::Status launch_result` through the E.5.2 steps (`status_from_cuda` -> `kInvalid`, collapsed to `kErrorInternal` with the `cudaGetLastError` result, item 11). The header's trace printf helper is the macro `EXPLICIT_GEMM_TRACE_PRINTF`, which no file uses: every toggle-on print of the `.cu` (`TRACE_HOST`, `TRACE_HANG*`, `TRACE_B4`) sits inside `#if defined(CUTLASS_DEABSTRACTION_TRACE)` and calls `std::printf` directly.
13. **Discriminator**: the K1 payloads are the ten words of the CUTLASS probe (`sm100_gemm_tma_warpspecialized.hpp:620-640`), so section 1 of `check_trace.py` runs unchanged; the "K1 payload field the CUTLASS blocks never emit" of E.7.3 was not added. Section 7 discriminates on `TRACE_HOST explicit_trace 1` (printed by `trace_begin`) and on the presence of `K_TAIL` records (kind 13, never emitted by the CUTLASS kernel), and prints `explicit_kernel_name` as INFO.
14. **`inspect` binary**: `step_inspect` runs `cuobjdump` on `$EXPLICIT_BIN` (`$OUT/70_blackwell_fp16_gemm.explicit`, the copy saved by `build`), not on `$BIN` as E.7.3 says, so that a later toggle-on rebuild of `$BIN` cannot change the E.7.4/E.8 dumps.
15. **Toggle-on error print**: `run()` prints `cudaGetErrorString(result)` (line `TRACE_HOST explicit_run_launch_error`) only when `cudaGetLastError` or a step's `launch_result` failed; E.5.2 item 7 said it prints before every return. On the armed warm-up launch the watchdog's `TRACE_HOST explicit_hang_records ... cuda_error` line reports its own event-query/sync status instead.

Everything else follows Part E as written: the 33-init/4-fence variant, `tma_c` carried and prefetched, the literal `0.0f` C fragment, `fmaf(beta, 0.0f, alpha * acc)`, both attribute calls on every `run()`, the guards (including `beta != 0.0f -> kErrorNotSupported`), the hard-coded `(64, 64, 1)` grid, `#pragma unroll 1` on the three k-tile loops, full unrolling of the k-blocks, subtiles, registers, store boxes and tails, one `elect_one_sync()` per TMA pair / MMA / commit / query plus one per init group and the prologue, no `wait_group.read 0`.

## F.3 Review of the code (2026-09-11)

Two adversarial workflow passes read the three new files, the harness diff, the CMake edit and the trace-header edits against the note and the CUTLASS source at cb424739, without a compiler:

- Pass 1 (six lenses: PTX-string exactness, protocol/roles, compile/link for both toggle states, host bridge, trace/two-TU linkage, instruction shape and registers; 30-78 verified claims per lens; every finding adversarially verified). Confirmed defects, all fixed: the toggle-on harness called `explicit_gemm::trace_begin/trace_end` without a declaration visible to the harness TU (build break of the trace build only; declarations added to the `.hpp`); the K_TAIL payload comment contradicted the code (comment fixed, code kept: F.2 item 4); a dead external-linkage template named a TU-local static (removed); the heartbeat report filtered slots by fields the heartbeat never writes (now prints every slot). No PTX string, constraint, clobber, constant, offset, count, mask, parity, coordinate or role-order defect was found.
- Pass 2 (five attackers: a full barrier-protocol simulation of one cluster through two tiles and the exit; an element round trip A(m,k), B(k,n) -> smem -> descriptors -> TMEM -> registers -> swizzled smem -> TMA store -> D(m,n) for every rank and the 4096-tile coverage; the nvcc front end for the performance build; the nvcc front end and linker for the trace build; the host mirror plus the predicted E.7.4 inventory). No hang, fault, wrong-element, build or inventory defect was found; two low items were fixed (a local named `nan` renamed, the util header's include-list comment), and one hardening was applied (the suspend hint of the traced wait is a local, as in `ClusterBarrier::wait`). Refuted: an `asm` risk for a namespace-scope `constexpr` operand and a predicted unused-variable warning.
- Pass 3, final pre-compile inspection (seven lenses, 87/26/32/58/29/58/142 verified claims): a statement-by-statement walk of the baseline's beta = 0 path against the explicit kernel (every executed statement has a counterpart with the same thread set and operands; the omissions are exactly the recorded ones); process semantics (barrier counts, parities, masks, tails, CLC ordering, dealloc handshake); bit-exactness for all inputs (same MMA sequence, descriptors and `scale_c`; `alpha * acc` then `fmaf(beta, 0, t)` is the baseline's FMUL + FFMA with a zero C register); performance parity (no regression source; hot loops match the D.2 shape); abstraction-freedom (the 39-header closure of E.2.1, only `cutlass::Status`, the `cute::Swizzle` `static_assert`s and the host-side `cute::get`); compilability in both toggle states; note consistency. No defect in kernel semantics, result accuracy, performance or abstraction was found. Fourteen low/medium items were applied: `kStageBytesAHalf` for the A destination offset (no codegen change), the `status_from_cuda` and printf-macro comments, a dead clause in `check_sass.py`, the `Fatbin` reset of the SASS/PTX cuts and `diff -B` in the run script, the `all` order and B5 gate (F.5), and the note corrections recorded in F.2 items 12-15, E.2.1, E.3.3, E.4, E.5.2, E.7.2, E.7.4, C.5 and this table. Refuted: a claimed elect-region width mismatch in `load_ktiles` and a claimed set of unused constants.

## F.4 What only the first build and run can settle (carry into the report)

Build: the second TU receives the D.5 flag line (`flags.make`); `alignof(CUtensorMap)` and hence `sizeof(ExplicitGemmParams)` (576 or 640; `CONSTANT[0]` = 896 + that); the `cute::Swizzle<2,5,2>{}(0x1E4u)` constant expression in the `static_assert`; `cute/swizzle.hpp` and `cute/container/tuple.hpp` compiling standalone; two `sm_100a` cubins in `--list-elf`; the `-Wconversion` warnings (not errors). Run and inventory: `st.shared.b32` (not `.u32`/`.f32`) for the epilogue stores; `mul.f32`/`fma.rn.f32` 128/128 with `add.f32` 0; 4 `cp.async.bulk.tensor ... cta_group::2` (two rolled producer loops after inlining `load_ktiles` twice); `REG` (68 is the soft target); `LOCAL 0`, `STACK 0`, `SHARED 1024`; the dynamic smem base `0x400 + (rank << 24)` (K1) and TMEM base 0 (K2); `max_active_clusters` 33 for the explicit kernel (H3); the hang bound and pinned-buffer visibility after `__trap()` (only if something hangs); the `Avg runtime` tiers of E.8 C2 against the same-day pristine median.

## F.5 Bring-up procedure (E.7.2 B5 order; every command is the Section 0 build or run, driven by the script)

```
export CUDACXX=... CUDA_HOME=...            # CUDA 13.3 as in D.5
S=examples/70_blackwell_gemm/deabstraction_trace_run.sh
$S trace      # 1. toggle ON, build, one fixed run -> trace_out_v2/host.txt, launch0.csv; prints TRACE_HANG*/TRACE_B4/Disposition lines
$S check      # 2. python3 check_trace.py -> trace_out_v2/checklist.txt: expect 0 FAIL incl. section 7 (K_TAIL, no hangs, explicit_trace 1)
$S build      # 3. toggle OFF, build -> trace_out_v2/70_blackwell_fp16_gemm.explicit
$S inspect    # 4. cuobjdump on that binary; sass_explicit.txt, ptx_explicit.txt, sass_gemm.txt (== trace_out/sass_gemm.txt?), check_sass.py -> sass_checklist.txt
$S baseline   # 5. 5 x pristine + 5 x explicit interleaved with the clock log -> baseline.txt, timing_summary.txt (E.8 C1-C3 tiers)
$S restore    # 6. toggle OFF rebuild; explicit kernel SASS identical to step 3's (E.8 D6)
```

The script's `all` target runs these six steps in this order and stops after step 2 (toggle still on, exit-trap warning) unless `host.txt` contains `Disposition: Passed` and `checklist.txt` reports `0 FAIL` (the B5 gate). Never run `baseline` before `trace` has passed: the bounded wait and the 20 s watchdog exist only in the toggle-on build, so a toggle-off hang has no bound (E.9 item 11).

If step 1 hangs, the run ends by itself: after 4 s every stuck warp writes a `TRACE_HANG site=... off=<Section 6.2 offset> parity=... t=<tile> q=<k-tile>` record and traps; the watchdog prints them (the first 64 records; the total count is in the `TRACE_HOST explicit_hang_records` line) and every `TRACE_HANG_HB` heartbeat slot after at most 20 s (E.7.2 B1/B2 and the failure-mode table of E.7.2). If step 1 prints `Disposition: Failed`, the `TRACE_B4` lines classify the mismatch (tile map, halves/quarters/subtiles of the first bad tile, `D/ref` ratio histogram, "tile holds the reference of tile" search). Steps 3-6 are the acceptance evidence of E.8; the report states the per-iteration host sequence (6 encodes + 1 attribute call on the CUTLASS kernel + 2 attribute calls on the explicit kernel + 1 launch + `cudaGetLastError`).

## F.6 Open decisions (unchanged from E.10; every default was implemented)

The user may still change: the file names; the guard policy and the `beta != 0` rejection; 33 vs 43 barrier inits; carrying/prefetching `tma_c`; the soft `REG` criterion; option B (re-encoding the maps in the replacement) as a later phase. Changing any of them is a local edit to the `.cu`/`.hpp` named in F.1.
